### Q1: What is a Surgical Safety Checklist (SSC)?

- **A)** A tool toreducetheirisk of preventable complications at surgery.
- **B)** A list of equipment that will be required during the surgery.
- **C)** A tool to ensure that all staff in the practice are prepared for an emergency situation.
- **D)** A document listing all the roles of staff members in the operating theatre.
- **E)** A tool for reviewing surgery outcome and implementing improvements.

**Correct Answer:** A
**Answer Source:** marked
**Source Pages:** 6

### Q2: How can the traditional surgical culture help improve safety in the operation theatre?

- **A)** Surgeon in-charge being responsible for the final outcome (hierarchical structure).

- **D)** The ability to work under stressful condition.

**Correct Answer:** D
